# Elements, components, and patterns

Web scan: **31 October 2026**. Input: [systems.md](src/resources/systems.md).

**2,296 consolidated names**, drawn from **5,263 source records** across **63 systems**. All **64 original website links** were attempted. The Economist could not be enumerated because its pages returned only a navigation shell or fetch errors.

This is one alphabetical bullet list. It covers named visual elements, layout primitives, component families, documented variants, interaction patterns, and page templates exposed by the accessible catalogs and official source repositories. Each bullet links to evidence. Spelling/spacing and common singular/plural variants are consolidated; distinct controls and named variants stay separate.

Coverage is broad but is **not a verified exhaustive crawl of every page or platform**. Apple component coverage and several JavaScript-only catalogs remain partial; the source audit below identifies fallbacks, version differences, and missing pattern libraries. Repository component names establish that a building block exists, but do not guarantee a stable public API or a published styleguide example. Individual icon glyphs, token values, API props, every compositional subpart, hooks, and general developer tooling are outside this names-level inventory.

- **your Gov example application** — Pattern. Sources: [Agriculture Design System](https://design-system.agriculture.gov.au/patterns).
- **Z-index** — Element. Sources: [U.S. Web Design System (USWDS)](https://designsystem.digital.gov/); [NSW Design System](https://designsystem.nsw.gov.au/components/content/index.html); [Bootstrap](https://getbootstrap.com/docs/5.3/components/accordion/) (v5.3); [Rivet](https://rivet.iu.edu/components/).
- **Z-Index Classes** — Component. Sources: [Gestalt](https://gestalt.pinterest.systems/v1/web/overview) (v1).

## Source coverage and access notes

Counts are distinct consolidated names per system, so one name appearing in several systems contributes to several rows. An accessible index can establish a name without the linked detail page having been individually read.

| System / original link | Names | Scan outcome |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| [Material Design 3](https://m3.material.io) | 39 | Original site and component index require JavaScript. Recovered Material Web and Android Compose catalogs; M3 Expressive website-only entries may be missing. |
| [Human Interface Guidelines](https://developer.apple.com/design) | 51 | Apple homepage, HIG, patterns data, and selected component data checked. Pattern index recovered; component collection unavailable, so component coverage is partial. |
| [Fluent 2](https://fluent2.microsoft.design) | 55 | Web, iOS, Android, and Windows landing pages checked. Named web/iOS/Android catalogs recovered; Windows page links to WinUI rather than listing controls. |
| [Carbon Design System](https://carbondesignsystem.com) | 51 | Core component and universal pattern indexes recovered. |
| [Atlassian Design System](https://atlassian.design) | 97 | Components, foundations, Rovo UI, and AMP overview checked. Deprecated components retained and marked. |
| [Polaris](https://shopify.dev) | 64 | shopify.dev redirects to developer docs. Current App Home web components and page templates/compositions recovered. |
| [Spectrum](https://spectrum.adobe.com) | 103 | Current website has a sparse rendered shell. Recovered official React Spectrum packages and Spectrum 2 public export names. |
| [Lightning Design System](https://lightningdesignsystem.com) | 91 | Original website inaccessible. Official Salesforce component blueprint directories recovered; current SLDS 2-only additions may be missing. |
| [Primer](https://primer.style) | 116 | Product UI components, internal components, UI patterns, scenario patterns, and visual primitives recovered. Internal entries are marked in their source context. |
| [SAP Fiori](https://sap.com/design-system) | 160 | Original URL failed. Recovered official SAPUI5 v1.136 UI-element catalog and current UI5 Web Components/Fiori/AI indexes; version contexts differ. |
| [Cloudscape](https://cloudscape.design) | 153 | Component and pattern catalogs recovered, including legacy chart entries. |
| [PatternFly](https://patternfly.org) | 186 | Original bare domain failed. Official www component overview recovered, including chart, topology, extension, and pattern navigation. |
| [Base](https://base.uber.com) | 80 | Original homepage has no readable content. Official uber/baseweb source components recovered. |
| [Elastic UI (EUI)](https://eui.elastic.co) | 119 | Topic catalogs, patterns, and official EUI component source directories recovered. |
| [Paste](https://www.twilio.com/docs/paste) | 81 | Original docs URL failed; legacy site redirects to GitHub. Official Twilio component packages recovered. Pattern documentation not fully recovered. |
| [Pajamas](https://design.gitlab.com) | 107 | Component, pattern, and product-foundation collections recovered. The novelty Socks entry was excluded as unrelated to interface building blocks. |
| [Garden](https://garden.zendesk.com) | 67 | Component and pattern indexes recovered, including named button/form/loader subcomponents. |
| [Gestalt](https://gestalt.pinterest.systems) | 81 | Homepage links to public v1 web overview. v1 catalog recovered; separate Gestalt 2.0 coverage was not established. |
| [Cedar](https://cedar.rei.com) | 60 | Homepage exposes components, foundation elements, and form/notification patterns. |
| [Backpack](https://www.skyscanner.design) | 99 | Website has no readable text. Official Backpack web component source directories recovered; native iOS/Android catalogs not included. |
| [Visa Product Design System](https://design.visa.com) | 63 | Component, pattern, and base-element catalogs recovered. |
| [Dialtone](https://dialtone.dialpad.com) | 72 | Component and foundation catalogs recovered; beta/new labels retained as status notes. |
| [Coral](https://design.talend.com) | 44 | Landing page links to unavailable design-system page. Official Talend Coral source components recovered. |
| [Morningstar Design System](https://designsystem.morningstar.com) | 73 | Component status and chart-element catalogs recovered. Pattern library links to Figma and was not enumerated. |
| [Porsche Design System](https://designsystem.porsche.com) | 74 | Original URL redirects to v4 and labels it an earlier release. v4 components, patterns, templates, and tokens recovered; experimental/deprecated markers preserved. |
| [The Economist Design System](https://design-system.economist.com) | 0 | Homepage, foundations/components/pattern indexes, specific paths, and old chart-guide URL return a navigation shell or fetch error. No component or pattern names could be verified; no names inferred. |
| [GOV.UK Design System](https://design-system.service.gov.uk) | 80 | Styles, component, and pattern indexes recovered. |
| [U.S. Web Design System (USWDS)](https://designsystem.digital.gov) | 75 | Homepage navigation exposes components, pattern families, tokens, utilities, and templates. |
| [Scottish Government Design System](https://designsystem.gov.scot) | 65 | Styles, component, and pattern indexes recovered. |
| [NHS Design System](https://service-manual.nhs.uk/design-system) | 60 | Styles, component, and pattern indexes recovered. |
| [GC Design System](https://design-system.canada.ca/en/) | 94 | Component names and foundation/CSS shortcut families recovered from official navigation. |
| [NSW Design System](https://designsystem.nsw.gov.au) | 76 | Category pages expose full component navigation; core styles, utilities, and methods also recovered. |
